  +                exchanged, translated (exact values, independent of the mock relaxation)."""
  +                ch = child0.copy()
  +                pos = {x: parent.positions[cp] + rel[x] for x in mol}
  +                if exchange:
  +                    for a_, b_ in zip(grp[0], grp[1]):
  +                        pos[a_], pos[b_] = pos[b_].copy(), pos[a_].copy()
  +                P = np.array([pos[x] for x in mol])
  +                if deg:
  +                    P = _rotate_about(P, parent.positions[cp], (0.3, 0.5, 0.81), deg)
  +                for x, q in zip(mol, P):
  +                    ch.positions[p2c[x]] = q + shift * u
  +                return ch
  +
  +            def ino_case(dr):
  +                ch = child0.copy()
  +                ch.positions[ino[0]] = parent.positions[c2p[ino[0]]] + dr * u
  +                return ch
  +
  +            def run(nm, child, reading):
  +                fp = write_cell(os.path.join(outd, f"inv_{kind}_{nm}.extxyz"), child)
  +                job = dict(va, label=f"s2r_m30_Cs188o0_{leg}", kind=kind, site=128, token="Cs", leg=leg,
  +                           out=os.path.join(outd, f"inv_{kind}_{nm}_{reading}.json"),
  +                           files={"host_relaxed": cf(hw, "relaxed"), "region": Ly.region("c1", "ref233", "A", 128),
  +                                  "pair": Ly.pair("c1", "ref233", "Cs", 128, 0), "start": cf(w, "start"),
  +                                  "final": os.path.join(outd, f"inv_{kind}_{nm}.extxyz"), "parent_final": cf(par_w, "relaxed")})
  +                if reading is not None:
  +                    job["check5_reading"] = reading
  +                return run_job(job), fp
  +
  +            cases = (("unperturbed", child0, True, True), ("fa_base", fa_case(), True, True),
  +                     ("exchanged", fa_case(exchange=True), False, True), ("rot20.1", fa_case(20.1), False, False),
  +                     ("rot19.9", fa_case(19.9), True, True), ("carbon0.26_exchanged", fa_case(exchange=True, shift=0.26),
  +                                                               False, False),
  +                     ("carbon0.30_exchanged", fa_case(exchange=True, shift=0.30), False, False),
  +                     ("rot25", fa_case(25.0), False, False), ("inorganic0.30", ino_case(0.30), False, False))
  +            for nm, child, want_w, want_i in cases:
  +                o_def, fp = run(nm, child, None)
  +                o_w, _ = run(nm, child, "as_written")
  +                o_i, _ = run(nm, child, "invariant")
  +                vd, vw_, vi = o_def["validity"], o_w["validity"], o_i["validity"]
  +                assert canon(vd) == canon(vw_) and canon({**o_def, "written": 0, "out": 0}) == \
  +                    canon({**o_w, "written": 0, "out": 0}), f"{kind} {nm}: the default is not 'as_written'"
  +                for k_ in ("check5_reading", "passed_as_written", "rot_exchanged_deg", "invariant_only", "fa_needs_exchange",
  +                           "max_rot_fa_invariant_deg", "fa_rotation_invariant"):
  +                    assert k_ not in json.dumps(vd), f"{kind} {nm}: the default output has {k_}"
  +                c5 = vi["checks"]["5"]
  +                assert vd["checks"]["5"]["passed"] is want_w and c5["passed_as_written"] is want_w, (kind, nm, "as written")
  +                assert c5["passed"] is want_i and vi["valid"] is want_i and vi["valid_as_written"] is want_w and \

  +                assert vi["check5_reading"] == "invariant" and "check 5 read invariantly" in o_i["rule"]
  +                assert vi["invariant_only"] is (want_i and not want_w), (kind, nm, vi["invariant_only"])
  +                assert (5 in vi["failed"]) is (not want_i) and (5 in vi["failed_as_written"]) is (not want_w), (kind, nm)

  +                if nm == "rot25":                        # above the bound as written and exchanged

  +                r5 = d5.d5_check(kind, fp, parent, host, reg, built)       # the read-back final
  +                assert r5["passed"] is want_w and r5["passed_exchanged"] is want_i, (kind, nm, r5["passed"],
  +                                                                                    r5["passed_exchanged"])

  +FR_KINDS = ("end_rule", "check", "check_not_evaluated", "missing", "not_started_cap", "killed_by_gate", "not_run_gate")

  +def fr_progress(rows, *, final=False):
  +    """Order and early stop of FR (S2B_PLAN.md §2; S2B-2), a pure function. rows: one per FR cell in FR_ORDER,
  +    {"label", "status" (A2 record status or None), "end_rule" (outcome or None), "check" (the a2v job state or None),
  +    "valid" (validity_final.valid or None)}. The first cell that has not ended is the next one to run (final: a
  +    refusal); the first cell that ended without acceptance, without a final-geometry check or with a failed check ends
  +    FR. Returns {first_failure: None | {cell, kind, end_rule}, next, cells_not_run, passed}."""
  +    labs = [(r or {}).get("label") for r in rows]
  +    if labs != list(FR_ORDER):
  +        raise GateRefused(f"FR rows {labs} are not the FR cells in their order {list(FR_ORDER)} (S2B §2)")
  +    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
  +        lab, st = r["label"], r.get("status")
  +        kind = None
  +        if st is None or st == "pending":
  +            if final:
  +                raise GateRefused(f"FR: {lab} has not ended (status {st}); gate FR is evaluated at the end (S2B §2)")
  +            return {"first_failure": None, "next": lab, "cells_not_run": [], "passed": None}
  +        if st != "complete":
  +            kind = st
  +        elif r.get("end_rule") not in RULES["END_ACCEPTED"]:
  +            kind = "end_rule"
  +        elif r.get("valid") is None:
  +            if not final and r.get("check") in (None, "pending"):
  +                return {"first_failure": None, "next": None, "cells_not_run": [], "passed": None}   # the check waits
  +            kind = "check_not_evaluated"
  +        elif r.get("valid") is not True:
  +            kind = "check"
  +        if kind is not None:
  +            if kind not in FR_KINDS:
  +                raise GateRefused(f"FR: {lab} has the unknown A2 status {st!r}")
  +            return {"first_failure": {"cell": lab, "kind": kind, "end_rule": r.get("end_rule")}, "next": None,
  +                    "cells_not_run": list(FR_ORDER[i + 1:]), "passed": False}
  +    return {"first_failure": None, "next": None, "cells_not_run": [], "passed": True}
  +
  +
  +def fr_eval(t):
  +    """FR-1 (S2B_PLAN.md §2; S2B-X3, X4). t: {label: {"status", "end_rule" (outcome), "validity_final" (the record's
  +    leaves or None), "a1_valid"}} for the FR cells. A validity_final of another check-5 reading is a refusal. Returns
  +    {cells, first_failure, end_rule_failed, checks_failed (K3-style reasons), cells_not_run, invariant_only (labels),
  +    a1_invalid_reported, FR1}; the A1 validity of the start cells is reported only (I-11)."""
  +    rows, inv_only = [], []
  +    for lab in FR_ORDER:
  +        c = t.get(lab) or {}
  +        vf = c.get("validity_final")
  +        if vf is not None and vf.get("check5_reading") != "invariant":
  +            raise GateRefused(f"FR: {lab} has a final-geometry check without the invariant reading of check 5 "
  +                              f"(check5_reading {vf.get('check5_reading')!r}; S2B §2, X4)")
  +        rows.append({"label": lab, "status": c.get("status"), "end_rule": c.get("end_rule"),
  +                     "check": "done" if vf is not None else None,
  +                     "valid": None if vf is None else vf.get("valid") is True})
  +        if vf is not None and vf.get("invariant_only") is True:
  +            inv_only.append(lab)
  +    pr = fr_progress(rows, final=True)
  +    ff, end_bad, checks_bad = pr["first_failure"], [], []
  +    if ff is not None:
  +        c = t.get(ff["cell"]) or {}
  +        if ff["kind"] == "check":
  +            checks_bad.append(f"{ff['cell']}: final geometry fails check(s) {(c.get('validity_final') or {}).get('failed')}")
  +        elif ff["kind"] == "check_not_evaluated":
  +            end_bad.append(f"{ff['cell']}: no final-geometry check")
  +        else:
  +            end_bad.append(f"{ff['cell']}: end rule {c.get('end_rule')} (status {c.get('status')})")
  +    return {"cells": t, "first_failure": ff, "end_rule_failed": end_bad, "checks_failed": checks_bad,
  +            "cells_not_run": pr["cells_not_run"], "invariant_only": inv_only,
  +            "a1_invalid_reported": [lab for lab in FR_ORDER if (t.get(lab) or {}).get("a1_valid") is not True],
  +            "FR1": pr["passed"] is True}
  +
  +
  +def fr2_decide(delta_meV):
  +    """FR-2 (S2B_PLAN.md §2): |delta_far(FA188)| <= 50 meV; a missing value fails."""
  +    return delta_meV is not None and abs(delta_meV) <= RULES["FR2_MAX_MEV"]
  +
  +
  +def fr2_eval(F):
  +    """F: {label: {"F_k0_eV", "F_final_eV"}} of the FR cells. delta_far = E_b^A2 - E_b^A1 with E_b^A1 from the k = 0 F
  +    and E_b^A2 from the final F (P:618-621); a missing value is a refusal (S2B-X3), whose text names the cell and the
  +    configuration (k = 0 or final), never an energy field name or a value (the run CLI prints it to gates.log)."""
  +    miss = [f"{lab} {'k = 0' if k == 'F_k0_eV' else 'final'} F" for lab in FR_ORDER for k in ("F_k0_eV", "F_final_eV")
  +            if (F.get(lab) or {}).get(k) is None]
  +    if miss:
  +        raise GateRefused(f"FR-2: values missing ({', '.join(miss)}): FR-2 cannot be evaluated (S2B §2)")
  +    k0 = {r: F[lab]["F_k0_eV"] for r, lab in FR_QUARTET.items()}
  +    fin = {r: F[lab]["F_final_eV"] for r, lab in FR_QUARTET.items()}
  +    a1 = eb_meV(k0["DV"], k0["D"], k0["V"], k0["host"])
  +    a2 = eb_meV(fin["DV"], fin["D"], fin["V"], fin["host"])
  +    d = a2 - a1
  +    return {"Eb_A1_meV": a1, "Eb_A2_meV": a2, "delta_far_meV": d, "bound_meV": RULES["FR2_MAX_MEV"],
  +            "passed": fr2_decide(d)}
  +

  +# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2b budget (S2B §8; amendment 2026-11-16a)
  +def plan_work_s2b(state, point):
  +    """C3B-2: the work still planned at an S2b projection point. Both points precede every c3b job, so the whole of
  +    C3b is planned: n = 8 hosts at default poses without Ca (S2B §3), with 68 single points, 67 frozen relaxations and
  +    one host relaxation per host; and no A2 relaxation, since C4b is not run (C4B_PLANNED). A state that does not show
  +    n = 8, Ca removed and Cl kept by G1, and default poses is refused, and so is a planned C4b (not implemented)."""
  +    if point not in S2B_POINTS:
  +        raise GateRefused(f"unknown S2b projection point {point!r} (one of {', '.join(S2B_POINTS)})")
  +    if C4B_PLANNED:
  +        raise GateRefused("C4b is planned (C4B_PLANNED): its relaxations, allowance and cut order are not implemented "
  +                          "at this commit (amendment 2026-11-16a)")
  +    state = state or {}
  +    g1, pose = state.get("G1") or {}, state.get("pose") or {}
  +    bad = ([f"n = {state.get('n')!r}"] if state.get("n") != RULES["N_BASE"] else []) + \
  +          ([f"G1.Ca = {g1.get('Ca')!r}"] if g1.get("Ca") is not False else []) + \
  +          ([f"G1.Cl = {g1.get('Cl')!r}"] if g1.get("Cl") is not True else []) + \
  +          [f"pose.{t} = {pose.get(t)!r}" for t in ("Cs", "Cl") if pose.get(t) != "default"]
  +    if bad:
  +        raise GateRefused(f"the state does not show the C3b of S2B §3 (n = 8, no Ca, default poses): {'; '.join(bad)}")
  +    per_sp = RULES["C3_PER_HOST"]["sp"] - RULES["C3_G1_REMOVAL"]["Ca"]["sp"]
  +    per_rx = RULES["C3_PER_HOST"]["relax"] - RULES["C3_G1_REMOVAL"]["Ca"]["relax"]
  +    n = RULES["N_BASE"]
  +    return {"N_relax": 0, "N_SP": n * per_sp, "N_MACE": n * per_rx, "host_waves": 1}

  +def projection_s2b(t):
  +    """C3B-2 (S2B §8; amendment 2026-11-16a): P of §16 (projection, without an A2 term) and U = the sum over the A2
  +    campaigns not yet ended of max(0, A_c - spent_c), plus R = 0.05 (P - H_spent). With fr ended and C4b not run no
  +    A2 campaign is left, so U = R. over_cap iff P + U > 520 node-h. Only counts and timings enter, never an energy."""
  +    if t.get("N_relax"):
  +        raise GateRefused("an S2b projection with A2 relaxations planned is not implemented (C4b is not run; "
  +                          "amendment 2026-11-16a)")
  +    p = projection({**t, "a2_completed": []})
  +    P, H = p["P_h"], float(t["H_spent_h"])
  +    allowance = 0.0                                  # no A2 campaign not yet ended (fr ended; C4b not run)
  +    R = S2B_RESERVE * (P - H)
  +    U = allowance + R
  +    return {"P_h": P, "U_h": U, "R_h": R, "allowance_h": allowance, "PU_h": P + U,
  +            "over_cap": P + U > RULES["HARD_CAP_H"], "terms": p["terms"], "tau_SP_h": p["tau_SP_h"],
  +            "mace_h_per_relax": p["mace_h_per_relax"], "host_wave_h": p["host_wave_h"], "counts": p["counts"]}

  +# ---------------------------------------------------------------- c3b: valid hosts and the A1 tier (C3B-3)
  +def c3b_sites_check(sites, state):
  +    """C3B-3: the site table scripts/50 passes, {host: {L: {"first": [...], "far": [...]}}}, names exactly the eight
  +    production hosts of the state, each with 2 / 3 / 8 first-shell sites (B / A / X) and two far sites per
  +    sublattice, no site twice; else refused."""
  +    prod = ((state or {}).get("stage0") or {}).get("production_n8")
  +    if not isinstance(sites, dict) or not sites:
  +        raise GateRefused("c3b_tier: no site table (inputs c1.c3b_sites)")
  +    why = []
  +    if not prod or len(prod) != RULES["N_BASE"]:
  +        why.append("the state holds no Stage-0 production list of 8 hosts")
  +    elif sorted(sites) != sorted(prod):
  +        why.append(f"its hosts {sorted(sites)} are not the production list {sorted(prod)}")
  +    for h in sorted(sites):
  +        for L in "BAX":
  +            row = (sites[h] or {}).get(L) or {}
  +            first, far = list(row.get("first") or []), list(row.get("far") or [])
  +            if len(first) != RULES["FIRST_SHELL_SIZE"][L] or len(far) != C3B_FAR_SITES \
  +                    or len(set(first + far)) != len(first) + len(far):
  +                why.append(f"{h} {L}: {len(first)} first-shell and {len(far)} far sites")
  +    if why:
  +        raise GateRefused(f"c3b_tier: the site table is not that of S2B §3: {'; '.join(why)}")
  +

  +def c3b_quartets(sites):
  +    """C3B-3: the production quartets of the site table, 24 per host (Sr at 4 B sites, Cs and GA at 5 A sites, Cl at
  +    10 X sites), each with the labels and work ids of its four cells (default pose)."""
  +    rows = []
  +    for h in sorted(sites):
  +        for L in "BAX":
  +            for role in ("first", "far"):
  +                for j in sites[h][L][role]:
  +                    for d in TOKENS_OF[L]:
  +                        if d in C3B_DOPANTS:
  +                            rows.append({"h": h, "L": L, "j": int(j), "role": role, "d": d,
  +                                         "labels": {"host": lab_host(h), "V": lab_v(h, L, int(j)),
  +                                                    "D": lab_dop(h, d, int(j), "novac"),
  +                                                    "DV": lab_dop(h, d, int(j), "vac")},
  +                                         "wids": {"host": wid_host(h), "V": wid_v(h, L, int(j)),
  +                                                  "D": wid_dop(h, d, int(j), "novac"),
  +                                                  "DV": wid_dop(h, d, int(j), "vac")}})
  +    return rows

  +def c3b_table_labels(sites):

  +    the table of S2B §3). scripts/50 compares them with the 'sp' labels of its graph before the first job."""
  +    return sorted({lab for q in c3b_quartets(sites) for lab in q["labels"].values()})

  +def c3b_tier_eval(hosts, quartets):
  +    """C3B-3, a pure function of validity flags. quartets: rows with h, d, role ('first' | 'far') and valid. A host
  +    is valid for d iff both far quartets of d are valid and at least VALID_HOST_MIN of its first-shell quartets on
  +    d's sublattice are (P:680-682); n_d counts them; computable iff n_d >= 2 for a decisive dopant."""
  +    n_d, valid_hosts = {}, {}
  +    for d in C3B_DOPANTS:
  +        L, ok = SUBLATTICE[d], []
  +        for h in sorted(hosts):
  +            far = [bool(q["valid"]) for q in quartets if q["h"] == h and q["d"] == d and q["role"] == "far"]
  +            first = [bool(q["valid"]) for q in quartets if q["h"] == h and q["d"] == d and q["role"] == "first"]
  +            if len(far) != C3B_FAR_SITES or len(first) != RULES["FIRST_SHELL_SIZE"][L]:
  +                raise GateRefused(f"c3b_tier: {h} {d} has {len(first)} first-shell and {len(far)} far quartets")
  +            if all(far) and sum(first) >= RULES["VALID_HOST_MIN"][L]:
  +                ok.append(h)
  +        valid_hosts[d], n_d[d] = ok, len(ok)
  +    return {"n_d": n_d, "valid_hosts": valid_hosts,
  +            "computable": any(n_d[d] >= C3B_TIER_MIN_HOSTS for d in C3B_DECISIVE)}
  +
  +
  +def c3b_tier(reader, sites, state):
  +    """C3B-3: gate c3b_tier. Reads, for every cell of every production quartet, its single point in status mode and
  +    its record through the blinded loader (validity leaves only); no energy."""
  +    c3b_sites_check(sites, state)
  +    cells = {}
  +
  +    def cell(role, label, wid):
  +        if wid not in cells:
  +            st = reader.sp_state(label).get("status")
  +            rec = reader.record(wid)
  +            v = (rec or {}).get("validity") or {}
  +            if rec is not None and role != "host" and v.get("check5_reading") != "invariant":
  +                raise GateRefused(f"c3b_tier: the record of {wid} does not hold the invariant reading of check 5 "
  +                                  f"(check5_reading {v.get('check5_reading')!r}; S2B §3, X4)")
  +            why = ([] if st == "complete" else [f"single point {st}"]) + \
  +                  (["no record"] if rec is None else [] if v.get("valid") is True
  +                   else [f"invalid (checks {v.get('failed')})"])
  +            cells[wid] = {"role": role, "why": why, "invariant_only": v.get("invariant_only") is True}
  +        return cells[wid]
  +
  +    rows, bad = [], []
  +    for q in c3b_quartets(sites):
  +        why = {r: cell(r, q["labels"][r], q["wids"][r])["why"] for r in ("host", "V", "D", "DV")}
  +        valid = not any(why.values())
  +        rows.append({"h": q["h"], "d": q["d"], "role": q["role"], "valid": valid})
  +        if not valid:
  +            bad.append({"h": q["h"], "d": q["d"], "L": q["L"], "j": q["j"], "role": q["role"],
  +                        "cells": {q["wids"][r]: w for r, w in why.items() if w}})
  +    res = c3b_tier_eval(sorted(sites), rows)
  +    inv = sorted(w for w, c in cells.items() if c["invariant_only"])
  +    res.update({"n_quartets": len(rows), "n_valid_quartets": sum(r["valid"] for r in rows), "invalid_quartets": bad,
  +                "invariant_only": inv,
  +                "invariant_only_counts": {k: sum(1 for w in inv if cells[w]["role"] == k) for k in ("V", "D", "DV")}})
  +    return res
  +

  +                json.dump(rec, open(logical_path(lay, f"a2:{lab}"), "w"))
  +                put_rec(lay, a1_start_of(lab))
  +        put_sp(lay, sp_v, Fb["V"] + 0.0004)
  +        put_sp(lay, sp_dv, Fb["DV"] - 0.0005)
  +        plant(49.0)
  +        planted = {"step0": {"attempts": 1, "outcome": "pass", "repeat": False, "fix_note": None}, "K3": "fail_replan"}
  +        st = copy.deepcopy(planted)
  +        g0 = evaluate_gate("fr_step0", lay, "fr", st, inputs={"cells": [sp_v], "attempt": 1}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert set(g0["decision"]) == {"fr_step0"} and st == planted, g0["decision"]
  +        assert g0["decision"]["fr_step0"]["outcome"] == "pass" and g0["decision"]["fr_step0"]["cell"] == FR_QUARTET["V"]
  +        assert abs(g0["result"]["cells"][sp_v]["dF_meV"] + 0.4) < 1e-6              # F(k = 0) - F(SP)
  +        st.update(copy.deepcopy(g0["decision"]))
  +        g1 = evaluate_gate("fr_step0", lay, "fr", st, inputs={"cells": [sp_dv], "attempt": 1}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert g1["decision"]["fr_step0"]["outcome"] == "pass" and g1["decision"]["fr_step0"]["cell"] == FR_QUARTET["DV"]
  +        assert st["step0"] == planted["step0"] and "step0" not in g1["decision"]
  +        put_sp(lay, sp_dv, Fb["DV"] - 0.0006)                                   # |dF| = 0.6 meV: fails
  +        gf = evaluate_gate("fr_step0", lay, "fr", st, inputs={"cells": [sp_dv], "attempt": 1}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert gf["decision"]["fr_step0"]["outcome"] == "fail_pending_fix", gf["decision"]
  +        put_sp(lay, sp_dv, Fb["DV"] - 0.0005)
  +        no_e = set(FR_ENERGY_FIELDS) | {"F_sp_eV", "F_k0_eV", "F_final_eV", "value_meV", "dF_200_180_meV"}
  +        for g_ in (g0, g1, gf):                                 # the fr_step0 decision holds no dF and no F (S2B §1)
  +            ks = set(re.findall(r'"([^"]+)":', json.dumps(g_["decision"])))
  +            assert not ks & no_e and set(g_["decision"]["fr_step0"]) <= \
  +                {"attempts", "outcome", "repeat", "fix_note", "cell", "attempt1", "no_error_found", "note"}, ks
  +        for bad in (["s2_m30_VB136"], [sp_v, sp_dv], [], None, sp_v):
  +            refused(lambda b=bad: evaluate_gate("fr_step0", lay, "fr", st, inputs={"cells": b}, reader_kw=kw))
  +        refused(lambda: evaluate_gate("step0", lay, "fr", st, inputs={"cells": [sp_v]}, reader_kw=kw))   # no C2 cell
  +        # one repeat for FR as a whole: after the repeat every later fr_step0 is attempt 2 (I-F3)
  +        st_rep = {"fr_step0": {"attempts": 1, "outcome": "repeat_pending", "repeat": True, "fix_note": "2026-11-20 fix",
  +                               "cell": FR_QUARTET["V"]}, "gate_context": {"at": FR_QUARTET["V"]}}
  +        inp, _ = derive_inputs("fr_step0", lay, st_rep, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert inp["attempt"] == 2 and inp["cells"] == [sp_v] and inp["fix_note"] == "2026-11-20 fix", inp
  +        gr = evaluate_gate("fr_step0", lay, "fr", st_rep, inputs=inp, reader_kw=kw)
  +        st_rep.update(copy.deepcopy(gr["decision"])); st_rep["gate_context"] = {"at": FR_QUARTET["DV"]}
  +        inp2, _ = derive_inputs("fr_step0", lay, st_rep, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert inp2["attempt"] == 2 and inp2["cells"] == [sp_dv] and gr["decision"]["fr_step0"]["repeat"], inp2
  +        refused(lambda: derive_inputs("fr_step0", lay, {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_final"}}, reader_kw=kw))
  +        assert derive_inputs("step0", lay, {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_final"}, "step0": {"outcome": "pass"}},
  +                             reader_kw=kw)[0]["attempt"] == 1                          # the C2 key is its own
  +        out.append("fr_step0: 0.5000 / 0.5001 meV, cross-set SPs refused both ways, only fr_step0 written (planted "
  +                   "step0 unchanged; no dF or F in a pass or fail decision), attempt 2 after the repeat")
  +
  +        # S2B-1: gates bound to campaigns, refused before anything is read (a layout that cannot be touched) or written
  +        class _NoRead:
  +            def __getattr__(self, k):
  +                raise AssertionError(f"the layout was used ({k}) before the gate-campaign refusal")
  +        nb = 0
  +        for gate, camp in [(g_, c_) for g_ in S2B_GATES for c_ in ("c1", "c2", "c3", "c4")] + \
  +                          [(g_, "fr") for g_ in GATE_NAMES if g_ not in S2B_GATES]:
  +            refused(lambda g_=gate, c_=camp: evaluate_gate(g_, _NoRead(), c_, None))
  +            nb += 1
  +        import contextlib, io
  +        cli = os.path.join(work, "cli_fr"); os.makedirs(cli)
  +        g_ = globals()                                  # the CLI refuses before scripts/46, the state or derive_inputs
  +        saved = {k: g_[k] for k in ("dep", "derive_inputs")}
  +
  +        def _no_input(*a_, **k_):
  +            raise AssertionError("the run CLI reached its inputs before the gate-campaign refusal (S2B-1)")
  +        try:
  +            g_.update(dep=_no_input, derive_inputs=_no_input)
  +            for args in (["--gate", "FR", "--campaign", "c2"], ["--gate", "fr_step0", "--campaign", "c1"],
  +                         ["--gate", "K3", "--campaign", "fr"], ["--gate", "step0", "--campaign", "fr"],
  +                         ["--gate", "projection", "--campaign", "fr"]):
  +                err = io.StringIO()
  +                with contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
  +                    rc = main(["run", "--root", cli, "--kind", "archive", "--state", os.path.join(cli, "none.json")] + args)
  +                assert rc == 3 and "S2B" in err.getvalue() and not os.listdir(cli), (args, rc, err.getvalue())
  +        finally:
  +            g_.update(saved)
  +        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(lay.gate("fr", "K3")), exist_ok=True)
  +        json.dump({"gate": "K3", "state_in": {}, "inputs_in": {}}, open(lay.gate("fr", "K3"), "w"))
  +        refused(lambda: verify(lay.root, "fr", layout=lay, reader_kw=kw))       # verify --campaign fr follows
  +        os.remove(lay.gate("fr", "K3"))
  +        out.append(f"gates bound to campaigns: {nb} pairings refused before the layout is touched; the run CLI exits 3 "
  +                   f"before scripts/46, the state or derive_inputs is reached (projection/fr among its 5 pairings) and "
  +                   f"writes nothing; verify --campaign fr refuses a K3 file")
  +
  +        # gate FR
  +        s_ok = {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "pass", "cell": FR_QUARTET["DV"]}, "K3": "fail_replan"}
  +        gp = evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_ok, reader_kw=kw)
  +        d = gp["decision"]["FR"]
  +        assert d == {"outcome": "pass", "FR1": True, "FR2": True, "part": None, "cell": None, "kind": None}, d
  +        assert abs(gp["result"]["FR2"]["delta_far_meV"] - 49.0) < 1e-6 and abs(gp["result"]["FR2"]["Eb_A1_meV"]) < 1e-6
  +        assert {o["mode"] for o in gp["opened"]} == {"files", "records", "k0", "final"}
  +        plant(51.0)
  +        d = evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_ok, reader_kw=kw)["decision"]["FR"]
  +        assert d["outcome"] == "fail" and d["part"] == "FR-2" and d["FR1"] is True and d["FR2"] is False and \
  +            d["cell"] is None, d
  +        assert fr2_decide(50.0) and fr2_decide(-50.0) and not fr2_decide(50.001) and not fr2_decide(-50.001) and \
  +            not fr2_decide(None)
  +        nf = 0
  +        for i, (r, lab) in enumerate(FR_QUARTET.items()):
  +            for kind, upd in fails.items():
  +                a2u = {r: {"status": upd["status"]} if "status" in upd else
  +                       {"end_rule": {"outcome": "unconverged_cap"}} if kind == "end_rule" else {}}
  +                # the readings differ: check 4 fails invariantly, checks 4 and 5 as written (checks_failed names [4])
  +                vfu = {r: {"valid": False, "failed": [4], "valid_as_written": False, "failed_as_written": [4, 5]}}
  +                if "status" in upd or kind == "check_not_evaluated":
  +                    a2u[r]["validity_final"] = None
  +                for r2 in list(FR_QUARTET)[i + 1:]:
  +                    a2u[r2] = {"status": "not_run_gate", "validity_final": None}
  +                plant(10.0, a2=a2u, vf=vfu if kind == "check" else None)
  +                for s_ in (s_ok, {}):
  +                    g = evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_, reader_kw=kw)
  +                    d = g["decision"]["FR"]
  +                    assert d == {"outcome": "fail", "FR1": False, "FR2": None, "part": "FR-1", "cell": lab,
  +                                 "kind": kind}, (lab, kind, d)
  +                    assert {o["mode"] for o in g["opened"]} <= {"files", "records"}, g["opened"]   # no energy opened
  +                    res = g["result"]
  +                    assert res["cells_not_run"] == list(FR_ORDER[i + 1:]) and res["FR2"] is None
  +                    if kind == "check":                 # the reason texts, exactly (E reads them; S2B section 1)
  +                        want = ([f"{lab}: final geometry fails check(s) [4]"], [])
  +                    elif kind == "check_not_evaluated":
  +                        want = ([], [f"{lab}: no final-geometry check"])
  +                    elif kind == "end_rule":
  +                        want = ([], [f"{lab}: end rule unconverged_cap (status complete)"])
  +                    else:
  +                        want = ([], [f"{lab}: end rule accepted_converged (status {kind})"])
  +                    assert (res["checks_failed"], res["end_rule_failed"]) == want, (lab, kind, res["checks_failed"],
  +                                                                                   res["end_rule_failed"])
  +                nf += 1
  +        plant(10.0, vf={"V": {"invariant_only": True, "valid_as_written": False, "failed_as_written": [5]}})
  +        g = evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_ok, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert "invariant_only" not in json.dumps(g["decision"]) and g["result"]["invariant_only"] == [FR_QUARTET["V"]]
  +        assert g["decision"]["FR"]["outcome"] == "pass"                         # no validity leaf in the decision
  +        for bad in ({"check5_reading": None}, {"check5_reading": "as_written"}):
  +            plant(10.0, vf={"D": bad})
  +            refused(lambda: evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_ok, reader_kw=kw))
  +        plant(10.0)
  +        for s_ in ({"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_pending_fix", "cell": FR_QUARTET["V"]}},
  +                   {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_final", "cell": FR_QUARTET["DV"]}},
  +                   {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "pass", "cell": FR_QUARTET["V"]}}, {}):
  +            refused(lambda s=s_: evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s, reader_kw=kw))
  +        # a failed step-0 refuses gate FR by itself (X6), also when FR-1 fails (D+V killed after a step-0 failure at
  +        # @4), where the FR-2 precondition does not apply
  +        plant(10.0, a2={"DV": {"status": "killed_by_gate", "validity_final": None}})
  +        for outc in ("fail_pending_fix", "fail_final"):
  +            try:
  +                evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", {"fr_step0": {"outcome": outc, "cell": FR_QUARTET["DV"]}}, reader_kw=kw)
  +                raise AssertionError(f"gate FR evaluated at fr_step0 {outc}")
  +            except GateRefused as e:
  +                assert "X6" in str(e) and outc in str(e), e
  +        plant(10.0)
  +        os.remove(lay.a2_parts("fr", FR_QUARTET["D"])[0])                          # FR-2 value missing: a STOP
  +        refused(lambda: evaluate_gate("FR", lay, "fr", s_ok, reader_kw=kw))
  +        out.append(f"gate FR: delta 49 pass, 51 fails FR-2, FR-1 fails for {nf} cell x kind cases with no relaxation "
  +                   "opened and the exact reason texts (checks_failed from the invariant failed list), invariant_only in "
  +                   "the result only, a missing or other reading, a failed step-0 (X6, also with FR-1 failing) and FR-1 "
  +                   "without the step-0 pass at D+V refused, a missing FR-2 value refused; fr2_decide +-50.000 / +-50.001")
  +
  +        # energy-name canary: two evaluations with F perturbed non-uniformly; every changed leaf is an FR energy field
  +        def leaves(x, path=()):
  +            if isinstance(x, dict):
  +                for k, v in x.items():
  +                    yield from leaves(v, path + (str(k),))
  +            elif isinstance(x, list):
  +                for i_, v in enumerate(x):
  +                    yield from leaves(v, path + (f"[{i_}]",))
  +            else:
  +                yield path, x
  +        skip = ("opened", "inputs_digest", "written", "commit")
  +        changed = set()
  +        for gate, inp_, pert in (("FR", {}, {"host": 0.0011, "V": -0.0023, "D": 0.0007, "DV": 0.0041}),
  +                                 ("fr_step0", {"cells": [sp_v]}, {"V": 0.0001})):
  +            plant(10.0)
  +            put_sp(lay, sp_v, Fb["V"] + 0.0004)
  +            a_ = evaluate_gate(gate, lay, "fr", s_ok, inputs=inp_, reader_kw=kw)
  +            plant(10.0, jitter=pert, jitter_final={"host": -0.0009, "D": 0.0017})
  +            put_sp(lay, sp_v, Fb["V"] + 0.0004 - 0.00005)
  +            b_ = evaluate_gate(gate, lay, "fr", s_ok, inputs=inp_, reader_kw=kw)
  +            A_ = dict(leaves({k: v for k, v in a_.items() if k not in skip}))
  +            B_ = dict(leaves({k: v for k, v in b_.items() if k not in skip}))
  +            assert set(A_) == set(B_), gate
  +            diff = [p for p in A_ if A_[p] != B_[p]]
  +            assert diff and all(p[-1] in FR_ENERGY_FIELDS for p in diff), (gate, diff)
  +            changed |= {p[-1] for p in diff}
  +            assert a_["decision"] == b_["decision"], gate
  +        assert changed == set(FR_ENERGY_FIELDS), changed
  +        out.append(f"canary: the changed result leaves are {sorted(changed)} = FR_ENERGY_FIELDS")
  +
  +        # the run CLI's output (gates_applied/gates.log on the node, which the runner's STOP sends the operator to)
  +        # holds the decision only: fr_step0 and FR records, passing and failing, whose result holds every withheld field
  +        # (s2b/guard.py WITHHELD_S2B: FR_ENERGY_FIELDS, the step-0 and FR-2 F and the D-C2-12 list) with a sentinel
  +        # value, the gate file written under a temporary root; and each GateRefused path of fr_step0 and FR (FR-2
  +        # included, also with one F of a cell missing and the other a sentinel) reached through the CLI with sentinel F
  +        # planted; no withheld name and no sentinel value is printed (S2B sections 0, 1)
  +        import contextlib, io
  +        names = set(no_e)
  +        sent = {f: -987654.25 - 1000.0 * i for i, f in enumerate(sorted(names))}
  +        res_s = dict(sent, cells={sp_v: dict(sent, passed=True), sp_dv: dict(sent, passed=True)},
  +                     FR2=dict(sent, passed=True), rows=[dict(sent)])
  +        F_s = {r: (-977654.25 - 1000.0 * i, -967654.25 - 1000.0 * i) for i, r in enumerate(FR_QUARTET)}  # k = 0, final
  +        SP_s = {sp_v: -957654.25, sp_dv: -956654.25}
  +        vals = list(sent.values()) + [v for p_ in F_s.values() for v in p_] + list(SP_s.values())
  +        words = sorted(names) + [repr(v) for v in vals] + [str(int(abs(v))) for v in vals]
  +
  +        def plant_s(**kw_):
  +            """The FR cells and the two step-0 SPs with the sentinel F (k = 0, final, SP), exactly."""
  +            plant(0.0, jitter={r: F_s[r][0] - Fb[r] for r in FR_QUARTET},
  +                  jitter_final={r: F_s[r][1] - F_s[r][0] for r in FR_QUARTET}, **kw_)
  +            for sp_, F_ in SP_s.items():
  +                put_sp(lay, sp_, F_)
  +        cli2 = os.path.join(work, "cli_fr_out"); os.makedirs(cli2)
  +        g_ = globals()
  +        saved = {k: g_[k] for k in ("evaluate_gate", "derive_inputs")}
  +        mode = {"result": None}
  +
  +        def eval_cli(gate, layout_, campaign, state, **k_):
  +            k_.pop("reader_kw", None)                   # the synthetic fr archive and its parsers
  +            rec = saved["evaluate_gate"](gate, lay, campaign, state, reader_kw=kw, **k_)
  +            if mode["result"] is not None:
  +                rec["result"] = copy.deepcopy(mode["result"])
  +            return rec
  +
  +        def derive_cli(gate, layout_, state, inputs=None, **k_):
  +            return saved["derive_inputs"](gate, lay, state, inputs, reader_kw=kw)
  +
  +        def cli(gate, state, inputs=None):
  +            stp, inp_p = os.path.join(work, "cli_state.json"), os.path.join(work, "cli_inputs.json")
  +            json.dump(state, open(stp, "w")); json.dump(inputs or {}, open(inp_p, "w"))
  +            so, se = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
  +            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(so), contextlib.redirect_stderr(se):
  +                rc = main(["run", "--gate", gate, "--campaign", "fr", "--root", cli2, "--kind", "archive", "--state", stp,
  +                           "--inputs", inp_p])
  +            text = so.getvalue() + se.getvalue()
  +            leak = sorted({w for w in words if w in text})
  +            assert not leak, (gate, state, leak, text)
  +            return rc, text
  +        n_ok, n_ref = 0, 0
  +        try:
  +            g_.update(evaluate_gate=eval_cli, derive_inputs=derive_cli)
  +            try:
  +                c46_cli = dep("c46")
  +            except MissingDependency:
  +                c46_cli = None
  +            if c46_cli is not None and hasattr(c46_cli, "Layout"):
  +                mode["result"] = res_s
  +                ctx0 = {"gate_context": {"at": FR_QUARTET["V"]}}
  +                rep0 = {**ctx0, "fr_step0": {"attempts": 1, "outcome": "repeat_pending", "repeat": True,
  +                                             "fix_note": "2026-11-20 fix", "cell": FR_QUARTET["V"]}}
  +                bad_d = {"vf": {"D": {"valid": False, "failed": [4], "valid_as_written": False,
  +                                      "failed_as_written": [4, 5]}},
  +                         "a2": {"DV": {"status": "not_run_gate", "validity_final": None}}}
  +                # the passing decisions and the failing ones (fr_step0 at 0.6 meV at attempt 1 and 2, FR-1 failing at D,
  +                # FR-2 at 51 meV): a failure prints the decision only, as a pass does (no D-C2-10 STOP mode; S2B §1)
  +                for gate, s_, delta, sp_off, pl, want in (
  +                        ("fr_step0", ctx0, 10.0, 0.0004, {}, {"outcome": "pass", "attempts": 1}),
  +                        ("FR", s_ok, 10.0, 0.0004, {}, {"outcome": "pass", "FR1": True, "FR2": True}),
  +                        ("fr_step0", ctx0, 10.0, 0.0006, {}, {"outcome": "fail_pending_fix", "attempts": 1}),
  +                        ("fr_step0", rep0, 10.0, 0.0006, {}, {"outcome": "fail_final", "attempts": 2, "repeat": True}),
  +                        ("FR", s_ok, 10.0, 0.0004, bad_d, {"outcome": "fail", "FR1": False, "FR2": None, "part": "FR-1",
  +                                                           "cell": FR_QUARTET["D"], "kind": "check"}),
  +                        ("FR", s_ok, 51.0, 0.0004, {}, {"outcome": "fail", "FR1": True, "FR2": False, "part": "FR-2"})):
  +                    plant(delta, **pl)

  +                    put_sp(lay, sp_dv, Fb["DV"] - 0.0005)

  +                    dl = [x for x in text.splitlines() if x.startswith(f"{gate}: decision ")]
  +                    # R4-1: the whole captured output (stdout and stderr) is the one decision line, exactly: a print
  +                    # from inside the evaluation (a failing step-0 dF, delta_far) is caught however it is worded
  +                    exp = [f"{gate}: decision {json.dumps(json.loads(body)['decision'])}; written {gf}"]
  +                    assert rc == 0 and text.strip().splitlines() == exp and dl == exp and \
  +                        all(repr(v) in body for v in sent.values()), \
  +                        (gate, rc, text)                # the sentinels reach the gate file, not the printed line
  +                    dec = json.loads(dl[0][len(f"{gate}: decision "):].split("; written ")[0])
  +                    key = "fr_step0" if gate == "fr_step0" else "FR"
  +                    assert list(dec) == [key] and all(dec[key].get(k) == v for k, v in want.items()), (gate, dec, want)
  +                    os.remove(gf)
  +                    n_ok += 1
  +            mode["result"] = None
  +            V, DV, D = FR_QUARTET["V"], FR_QUARTET["DV"], FR_QUARTET["D"]
  +            ctxV = {"gate_context": {"at": V}}
  +            # each with its whole refusal text (R4-1: the captured output is that one line and nothing else)
  +            rows_ = [("fr_step0", {**ctxV, "fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_final", "cell": V}}, None, {},
  +                      "step-0 failed finally: A2 has stopped (§11)"),
  +                     ("fr_step0", {**ctxV, "fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_pending_fix", "cell": V}}, None, {},
  +                      "step-0 failed: A2 waits for the dated fix note (§11)"),
  +                     ("fr_step0", ctxV, {"attempt": 2}, {},
  +                      "step-0 attempt 2 given, the state implies attempt 1 (§11)"),
  +                     ("fr_step0", ctxV, {"cells": [sp_v, sp_dv]}, {},
  +                      f"fr_step0 needs exactly one SP of {sorted([sp_v, sp_dv])}, not {[sp_v, sp_dv]!r} (S2B §2)"),
  +                     ("fr_step0", {**ctxV, "fr_step0": {"outcome": "repeat_pending", "repeat": True, "fix_note": None,
  +                                                        "cell": V}}, None, {},
  +                      "step-0 repeat without the dated fix note (§11)"),
  +                     ("FR", {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_pending_fix", "cell": DV}}, None, {},
  +                      "gate FR: the FR step-0 check is fail_pending_fix; FR is not evaluated (S2B §2, X6)"),
  +                     ("FR", {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "fail_final", "cell": DV}}, None, {},
  +                      "gate FR: the FR step-0 check is fail_final; FR is not evaluated (S2B §2, X6)"),
  +                     ("FR", {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "pass", "cell": V}}, None, {},
  +                      f"gate FR: FR-1 passed without the step-0 pass at {DV} (fr_step0 pass at {V}; S2B §2)"),
  +                     ("FR", s_ok, None, {"missing": "D"},
  +                      f"FR-2: values missing ({D} k = 0 F, {D} final F): FR-2 cannot be evaluated (S2B §2)"),
  +                     ("FR", s_ok, None, {"final_missing": "D"},
  +                      f"FR-2: values missing ({D} final F): FR-2 cannot be evaluated (S2B §2)"),
  +                     ("FR", s_ok, None, {"a2": {"D": {"status": "pending"}}},
  +                      f"FR: {D} has not ended (status pending); gate FR is evaluated at the end (S2B §2)"),
  +                     ("FR", s_ok, None, {"a2": {"D": {"status": "bogus"}}},
  +                      f"FR: {D} has the unknown A2 status 'bogus'"),
  +                     ("FR", s_ok, None, {"vf": {"D": {"check5_reading": None}}},
  +                      f"FR: {D} has a final-geometry check without the invariant reading of check 5 (check5_reading "
  +                      f"None; S2B §2, X4)")]
  +            for gate, s_, inp_, pl, word in rows_:
  +                plant_s(**{k: v for k, v in pl.items() if k not in ("missing", "final_missing")})
  +                if "missing" in pl:
  +                    os.remove(lay.a2_parts("fr", FR_QUARTET[pl["missing"]])[0])        # its k = 0 and final F
  +                if "final_missing" in pl:                           # its final F only; its k = 0 F (a sentinel) stays
  +                    p_ = lay.a2_parts("fr", FR_QUARTET[pl["final_missing"]])[-1]
  +                    with gzip.open(p_, "rt") as fh:
  +                        d_ = json.load(fh)
  +                    d_["F_final_eV"] = None
  +                    with gzip.open(p_, "wt") as fh:
  +                        json.dump(d_, fh)
  +                rc, text = cli(gate, s_, inp_)
  +                assert rc == 3 and text.strip().splitlines() == [f"REFUSED: GateRefused: {word}"], \
  +                    (gate, s_, inp_, word, text)
  +                assert not os.path.exists(os.path.join(cli2, "fr", "gates", f"{gate}.json")), (gate, word)
  +                n_ref += 1
  +        finally:
  +            g_.update(saved)
  +            plant(10.0)
  +            for sp_, F_ in ((sp_v, Fb["V"] + 0.0004), (sp_dv, Fb["DV"] - 0.0005)):
  +                put_sp(lay, sp_, F_)
  +        assert n_ref == 13 and n_ok in (0, 6), (n_ok, n_ref)
  +        out.append(f"run CLI: {n_ok} fr runs (fr_step0 pass, fail_pending_fix, fail_final; FR pass, FR-1 and FR-2 fail) "
  +                   f"whose result holds the {len(names)} withheld fields (sentinels in the gate file) and {n_ref} "
  +                   f"GateRefused paths of fr_step0 and FR with sentinel F (one with only a final F missing) print no "
  +                   f"withheld name or value; each prints exactly one line, its decision line or its refusal text")
  +
  +        # verify --campaign fr: every file reproduces; a changed output gives 1 differing. IX-8: the output becomes the
  +        # public fr_verify/verify_fr.txt before E, so, as R4-1 pins the run CLI, the whole captured output (stdout and
  +        # stderr) of both runs is pinned line for line, with the sentinel F planted, and no withheld name or sentinel
  +        # value is printed; a print from the evaluation that verify runs is caught (a negative control)
  +        plant_s()
  +        st_v = {"fr_step0": {"outcome": "pass", "cell": FR_QUARTET["V"]}, "K3": "fail_replan"}
  +        recs = [("fr_step0", st_v, {"cells": [sp_dv], "attempt": 1}), ("FR", s_ok, {})]
  +        for gate, s_, inp_ in recs:
  +            rec = evaluate_gate(gate, lay, "fr", s_, inputs=inp_, reader_kw=kw)
  +            rec["state_in"], rec["inputs_in"], rec["c1_in"] = s_, inp_, None
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(lay.gate("fr", gate)), exist_ok=True)
  +            json.dump(rec, open(lay.gate("fr", gate), "w"))
  +
  +        def verify_out(want_rc, want):
  +            so, se = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
  +            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(so), contextlib.redirect_stderr(se):
  +                rc = verify(lay.root, "fr", layout=lay, reader_kw=kw)
  +            text = so.getvalue() + se.getvalue()
  +            leak = sorted({w for w in words if w in text})
  +            assert rc == want_rc and text.splitlines() == want and not leak, (rc, text, leak)
  +        ok_lines = ["OK   fr FR", "OK   fr fr_step0", "verify fr: 2 gate files, 0 differ"]
  +        verify_out(0, ok_lines)
  +        saved_v = g_["evaluate_gate"]
  +
  +        def eval_print(gate, *a_, **k_):                # the negative control: a print reached through verify
  +            rec_ = saved_v(gate, *a_, **k_)
  +            if gate == "FR":
  +                print({k: rec_["result"].get(k) for k in ("FR2",)})
  +            return rec_
  +        try:
  +            g_["evaluate_gate"] = eval_print
  +            try:
  +                verify_out(0, ok_lines)
  +                raise RuntimeError("verify --campaign fr: a print of the FR result was not caught")
  +            except AssertionError:
  +                pass
  +        finally:
  +            g_["evaluate_gate"] = saved_v
  +        put_a2(lay, FR_QUARTET["D"], F_s["D"][0], F_s["D"][1] + 0.0101)    # a changed output read by gate FR only
  +        rec = json.load(open(logical_path(lay, f"a2:{FR_QUARTET['D']}")))
  +        rec["validity_final"] = {"valid": True, "failed": [], "check5_reading": "invariant", "valid_as_written": True,
  +                                 "failed_as_written": [], "invariant_only": False}
  +        json.dump(rec, open(logical_path(lay, f"a2:{FR_QUARTET['D']}"), "w"))
  +        verify_out(1, ["DIFF fr FR", "OK   fr fr_step0", "verify fr: 2 gate files, 1 differ"])
  +        out.append("verify --campaign fr with sentinel F: exactly 'OK   fr FR', 'OK   fr fr_step0', 'verify fr: 2 gate "
  +                   "files, 0 differ' on stdout and stderr; a changed final output: exactly 'DIFF fr FR', 'OK   fr "
  +                   "fr_step0', '... 1 differ'; no withheld name or sentinel printed; a print of the FR result through "
  +                   "verify caught (IX-8)")
  +        return "; ".join(out)
  +
  +    def t_c3b():
  +        """S2b production c3b (S2B_PLAN.md §§3, 8; amendment 2026-11-16a; C3B-1 to C3B-3), synthetic values only."""
  +        out = []
  +        lay = _TestLayout(os.path.join(work, "arch_c3b"))
  +        hosts = ["m06", "m02", "m16", "m12", "m32", "m26", "m22", "m23"]
  +        st = base_state(G1={"Cl": True, "Ca": False}, stage0={"production_n8": hosts})
  +        # C3B-1: the label map; its default is that of 0a3e8f0
  +        assert label_campaign("s2_m06_host") == "c3" and label_campaign("s2_m06_host", "c3b") == "c3b"
  +        assert label_campaign("s2_m30_host", "c3b") == "c1" and label_campaign("s2r_m30_VA188", "c3b") == "fr"
  +        assert label_campaign("s2r_m05_VB124", "c3b") == "c4" and is_blinded("s2_m06_host")
  +        assert not set(C3B_GATES) & set(GATE_NAMES) and len({x.lower() for x in GATE_NAMES + C3B_GATES}) == 16
  +        # C3B-2: the planned work, P + U and the cap
  +        assert C4B_PLANNED is False and C4B_ORIGIN == "decision (S2B amendment 2026-11-16a)"
  +        w = plan_work_s2b(st, "fr")
  +        assert w == plan_work_s2b(st, "c3b") == {"N_relax": 0, "N_SP": 544, "N_MACE": 536, "host_waves": 1}
  +        for bad in (base_state(), base_state(G1={"Cl": True, "Ca": False}, n=10),
  +                    base_state(G1={"Cl": False, "Ca": False}), {},
  +                    base_state(G1={"Cl": True, "Ca": False}, pose={"Cs": "multi", "Cl": "default"}),
  +                    base_state(G1={"Cl": True, "Ca": False}, pose={"Cs": "default", "Cl": "multi"})):
  +            refused(lambda b=bad: plan_work_s2b(b, "c3b"))
  +        for pt in ("c4b", "after4", "c3", None):
  +            refused(lambda q=pt: plan_work_s2b(st, q))
  +        g_ = globals()
  +        try:
  +            g_["C4B_PLANNED"] = True
  +            refused(lambda: plan_work_s2b(st, "c3b"))                              # a planned C4b is not implemented
  +        finally:
  +            g_["C4B_PLANNED"] = False
  +        tin = {"H_spent_h": 120.0, "tau_SP_h": 0.11, "mace_h_per_relax": 0.02, "host_wave_h": 2.5}
  +        pj = projection_s2b({**w, **tin})
  +        P = 120.0 + 544 * 0.11 + 536 * 0.02 + 2.5                                  # an independent recomputation
  +        assert abs(pj["P_h"] - P) < 1e-9 and abs(pj["R_h"] - 0.05 * (P - 120.0)) < 1e-9 and pj["allowance_h"] == 0.0
  +        assert abs(pj["U_h"] - pj["R_h"]) < 1e-12 and abs(pj["PU_h"] - (P + 0.05 * (P - 120.0))) < 1e-9
  +        assert pj["over_cap"] is False and pj["counts"]["N_relax"] == 0 and pj["terms"]["A2_h"] == 0.0
  +        pu = projection_s2b({**w, "H_spent_h": 120.0})                             # §16 unit costs without timings
  +        assert abs(pu["P_h"] - (120.0 + 544 * 6.74 / 60 + 536 * 2 / 60 + 3.3)) < 1e-9
  +        refused(lambda: projection_s2b({**w, **tin, "N_relax": 1}))
  +        none = {"N_relax": 0, "N_SP": 0, "N_MACE": 0, "host_waves": 0}
  +        assert projection_s2b({**none, "H_spent_h": 520.0})["over_cap"] is False   # P + U = 520 exactly: not over
  +        assert projection_s2b({**none, "H_spent_h": 520.001})["over_cap"] is True
  +        X = P - 120.0                                                              # P + U = H + 1.05 X
  +        assert projection_s2b({**w, **tin, "H_spent_h": 520.0 - 1.05 * X - 1e-6})["over_cap"] is False
  +        assert projection_s2b({**w, **tin, "H_spent_h": 520.0 - 1.05 * X + 1e-6})["over_cap"] is True
  +        assert projection_s2b({**w, **tin, "H_spent_h": 520.0 - X})["over_cap"] is True      # P alone at the cap
  +        g1 = evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", st, inputs={"point": "fr", **tin}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        want = {"points": ["fr"], "cuts": [], "c4b_run": False, "c4b_origin": C4B_ORIGIN}
  +        assert g1["decision"] == {"s2b_projection": want} and not g1["opened"] and g1["result"]["point"] == "fr"
  +        assert tuple(want) == S2B_PROJECTION_KEYS and g1["result"]["over_cap"] is False
  +        st2 = {**st, **copy.deepcopy(g1["decision"])}
  +        g2 = evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", st2, inputs={"point": "c3b", **tin, "H_spent_h": 121.0},
  +                           reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert g2["decision"] == {"s2b_projection": {**want, "points": ["fr", "c3b"]}}
  +        st3 = {**st, **copy.deepcopy(g2["decision"])}
  +        for s_, pt in ((st, "c3b"), (st2, "fr"), (st3, "fr"), (st3, "c3b")):       # the points in their order, once
  +            refused(lambda a=s_, b=pt: evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", a, inputs={"point": b, **tin},
  +                                                     reader_kw=kw))
  +        refused(lambda: evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", st, inputs={"point": "fr"}, reader_kw=kw))
  +        go = evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", st, inputs={"point": "fr", **tin, "H_spent_h": 500.0},
  +                           reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert go["result"]["over_cap"] is True and go["decision"] == g1["decision"]         # scripts/50 stops on it
  +        poisoned = {**st, "K3": "pass", "K2": "fail", "cuts": [{"cut": 8}], "c4_run": True, "multi_pose_cut": {"Cs": 1}}
  +        gp = evaluate_gate("s2b_projection", lay, "c3b", poisoned, inputs={"point": "fr", **tin}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert gp["decision"] == g1["decision"] and gp["result"] == g1["result"]             # no C2 key is read
  +        put_json(lay, "ledger:fr", {"complete": True, "H_spent_h": 116.25})
  +        inp, _ = derive_inputs("s2b_projection", lay, st, {"point": "fr"}, reader_kw=kw)
  +        assert inp["H_spent_h"] == 116.25 and inp["tau_SP_h"] is None and inp["host_wave_h"] is None
  +        refused(lambda: derive_inputs("s2b_projection", lay, st, {"point": "c3b"}, reader_kw=kw))   # no live ledger
  +        refused(lambda: derive_inputs("s2b_projection", lay, st, {"point": "c3"}, reader_kw=kw))
  +        refused(lambda: derive_inputs("projection", lay, st, {"point": "fr"}, reader_kw=kw))        # legacy points only
  +        put_json(lay, "ledger:fr", {"complete": False, "H_spent_h": 116.25})
  +        refused(lambda: derive_inputs("s2b_projection", lay, st, {"point": "fr"}, reader_kw=kw))
  +        out.append("s2b_projection: 544 / 536 / 1 and no A2 work; P + U recomputed; 520.000 not over, 520.001 over; "
  +                   "points fr then c3b, once each; decision {points, cuts [], c4b_run false, c4b_origin}; C2 keys "
  +                   "unread; H_spent from the fr ledger at the point fr")
  +        # C3B-1: gates bound to campaigns
  +        class _NoRead:
  +            def __getattr__(self, k):
  +                raise AssertionError(f"the layout was used ({k}) before the gate-campaign refusal")
  +        nb = 0
  +        for gate, camp in [(a, b) for a in C3B_GATES for b in ("c1", "c2", "c3", "c4", "fr")] + \
  +                          [(a, "c3b") for a in GATE_NAMES]:
  +            refused(lambda a=gate, b=camp: evaluate_gate(a, _NoRead(), b, None))
  +            nb += 1
  +        import contextlib, io
  +        cli = os.path.join(work, "cli_c3b"); os.makedirs(cli)
  +        for args in (["--gate", "c3b_tier", "--campaign", "fr"], ["--gate", "s2b_projection", "--campaign", "c3"],
  +                     ["--gate", "projection", "--campaign", "c3b"], ["--gate", "c3_complete", "--campaign", "c3b"]):
  +            err = io.StringIO()
  +            with contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
  +                rc = main(["run", "--root", cli, "--kind", "archive", "--state", os.path.join(cli, "none.json")] + args)
  +            assert rc == 3 and "REFUSED" in err.getvalue() and not os.listdir(cli), (args, rc, err.getvalue())
  +        out.append(f"gates bound to campaigns: {nb} pairings refused before the layout is touched, 4 by the run CLI")
  +        # C3B-3: completeness and the tier, on a synthetic c3b archive (records under c3b/, status-mode reads)
  +        sites = {h: {"B": {"first": [124, 136], "far": [76, 172]}, "A": {"first": [28, 92, 128], "far": [164, 188]},
  +                     "X": {"first": [3, 49, 53, 90, 102, 125, 126, 138], "far": [175, 186]}} for h in hosts}
  +        qs = c3b_quartets(sites)
  +        labs = sorted({x for q in qs for x in q["labels"].values()})
  +        wids = sorted({x for q in qs for x in q["wids"].values()})
  +        assert len(qs) == 8 * 24 and len(labs) == 544 and len(wids) == 544
  +        states = {}
  +        kw2 = {**kw, "sp_status": lambda d, lab: {"status": states.get(lab, "complete"), "missing_reason": None}}
  +
  +        def put_c3b(wid, valid=True, reading="invariant", inv_only=False):
  +            p_ = lay.record("c3b", wid)
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p_), exist_ok=True)
  +            v = {"valid": valid, "failed": [] if valid else [5]}
  +            if not wid.endswith("_host"):
  +                v.update({"check5_reading": reading, "valid_as_written": valid and not inv_only,
  +                          "failed_as_written": [] if valid and not inv_only else [5], "invariant_only": inv_only})
  +                if reading != "invariant":
  +                    v = {"valid": valid, "failed": [] if valid else [5]}
  +            json.dump({"schema": "s2-cell-1", "work_id": wid, "validity": v, "relax": {"E_mace_eV": -1234.5}},
  +                      open(p_, "w"))
  +        for w_ in wids:
  +            put_c3b(w_)
  +        c1in = {"c3b_labels": labs, "c3b_sites": sites}
  +        cc = evaluate_gate("c3b_complete", lay, "c3b", st, c1=c1in, reader_kw=kw2)
  +        assert cc["decision"] == {"c3b_complete": True} and cc["result"]["n"] == 544
  +        states[labs[0]] = "pending"
  +        assert evaluate_gate("c3b_complete", lay, "c3b", st, c1=c1in, reader_kw=kw2)["decision"] == {"c3b_complete": False}
  +        states.clear()
  +        for badl in (labs[:-1], labs + [labs[0]], []):
  +            refused(lambda b=badl: evaluate_gate("c3b_complete", lay, "c3b", st, c1={"c3b_labels": b}, reader_kw=kw2))
  +
  +        def tier():
  +            return evaluate_gate("c3b_tier", lay, "c3b", st, c1=c1in, reader_kw=kw2)
  +        t0_ = tier()
  +        assert t0_["decision"] == {"c3b_tier": {"computable": True, "n_d": {"Sr": 8, "Cl": 8, "Cs": 8, "GA": 8}}}
  +        assert tuple(t0_["decision"]["c3b_tier"]) == C3B_TIER_KEYS and t0_["result"]["n_valid_quartets"] == 192
  +        assert all(o["mode"] in ("status", "records") and o["file"].startswith("c3b" + os.sep) for o in t0_["opened"])
  +        assert "E_mace_eV" not in json.dumps(t0_) and "-1234.5" not in json.dumps(t0_)
  +        rd_ = Reader(lay, "c3b", allow_list("c3b_tier", st, c1in), **kw2)
  +        refused(lambda: rd_.sp("s2_m06_host", "full"), Blinded)                    # a c3b energy read stays blinded
  +        refused(lambda: Reader(lay, "c3b", {"full": {"s2_m06_host"}}, **kw2).sp("s2_m06_host"), Blinded)
  +        # a single point not complete, a missing record and an invalid record each invalidate the quartet
  +        states["s2_m06_Cl175o0_vac"] = "missing"                                   # a far quartet of Cl on m06
  +        t1 = tier()
  +        assert t1["decision"]["c3b_tier"]["n_d"] == {"Sr": 8, "Cl": 7, "Cs": 8, "GA": 8}
  +        assert [b["cells"] for b in t1["result"]["invalid_quartets"]] == [{"s2_m06_Cl175p0_vac": ["single point missing"]}]
  +        states.clear()
  +        os.remove(lay.record("c3b", "s2_m02_Sr124p0_novac"))                       # B first shell: 2 of 2 needed
  +        put_c3b("s2_m16_VA92", valid=False)                                        # A first shell: 2 of 3 suffice
  +        put_c3b("s2_m12_VA92", valid=False); put_c3b("s2_m12_VA28", valid=False)   # 1 of 3: not valid for Cs, GA
  +        for j in (3, 49):

  +        for j in (3, 49, 53):
  +            put_c3b(f"s2_m26_Cl{j}p0_vac", valid=False)                            # 5 of 8: not valid for Cl
  +        put_c3b("s2_m22_host", valid=False)                                        # an invalid host: no dopant
  +        t2 = tier()
  +        assert t2["decision"]["c3b_tier"]["n_d"] == {"Sr": 6, "Cl": 6, "Cs": 6, "GA": 6}, t2["decision"]
  +        vh = t2["result"]["valid_hosts"]
  +        assert "m02" not in vh["Sr"] and "m16" in vh["Cs"] and "m12" not in vh["GA"] and "m32" in vh["Cl"] \
  +            and "m26" not in vh["Cl"] and all("m22" not in v for v in vh.values())
  +        assert {"s2_m02_Sr124p0_novac": ["no record"]} in [b["cells"] for b in t2["result"]["invalid_quartets"]]
  +        put_c3b("s2_m23_VB76", inv_only=True)                                      # valid, invariant reading only
  +        t3 = tier()
  +        assert t3["result"]["invariant_only"] == ["s2_m23_VB76"] and t3["decision"] == t2["decision"]
  +        assert t3["result"]["invariant_only_counts"] == {"V": 1, "D": 0, "DV": 0}
  +        put_c3b("s2_m23_VB76", reading="as_written")                               # another reading is a refusal
  +        refused(tier)
  +        put_c3b("s2_m23_VB76")
  +        t4 = tier()
  +        assert t4["result"]["invariant_only"] == [] and t4["decision"] == t2["decision"]
  +        # the thresholds of the pure rule, and 'computable'
  +        def rows(nfar, nfirst, d, hs):
  +            L = SUBLATTICE[d]
  +            return [{"h": h, "d": d, "role": "far", "valid": i < nfar} for h in hs for i in range(2)] + \
  +                   [{"h": h, "d": d, "role": "first", "valid": i < nfirst} for h in hs
  +                    for i in range(RULES["FIRST_SHELL_SIZE"][L])]
  +
  +        def n_of(d, nfar, nfirst, hs=("a", "b")):
  +            others = [r for x in C3B_DOPANTS if x != d for r in rows(0, 0, x, hs)]
  +            return c3b_tier_eval(list(hs), rows(nfar, nfirst, d, hs) + others)
  +        for d, lo in (("Sr", 2), ("Cs", 2), ("GA", 2), ("Cl", 6)):
  +            assert n_of(d, 2, lo)["n_d"][d] == 2 and n_of(d, 2, lo - 1)["n_d"][d] == 0, d
  +            assert n_of(d, 1, RULES["FIRST_SHELL_SIZE"][SUBLATTICE[d]])["n_d"][d] == 0, d       # one far quartet lost
  +        assert n_of("Sr", 2, 2)["computable"] is False                             # Sr is not decisive
  +        assert n_of("Cl", 2, 8)["computable"] is True and n_of("Cl", 2, 8, hs=("a",))["computable"] is False
  +        refused(lambda: c3b_tier_eval(["a"], rows(2, 2, "Sr", ["a"])))             # quartets of a dopant missing
  +        # the site table
  +        for bad in (None, {}, {h: sites[h] for h in hosts[:7]}, {**sites, "m30": sites["m06"]},
  +                    {**sites, "m06": {**sites["m06"], "A": {"first": [28, 92], "far": [164, 188]}}},
  +                    {**sites, "m06": {**sites["m06"], "X": {"first": sites["m06"]["X"]["first"], "far": [175]}}},
  +                    {**sites, "m06": {**sites["m06"], "B": {"first": [124, 136], "far": [76, 124]}}}):
  +            refused(lambda b=bad: evaluate_gate("c3b_tier", lay, "c3b", st, c1={"c3b_sites": b}, reader_kw=kw2))
  +        refused(lambda: evaluate_gate("c3b_tier", lay, "c3b", base_state(), c1=c1in, reader_kw=kw2))   # no host list
  +        # verify reproduces the three c3b gate files, and names a changed record
  +        for rec_, sin, iin in ((g2, st2, {"point": "c3b", **tin, "H_spent_h": 121.0}), (cc, st, {"c1": c1in}),
  +                               (t4, st, {"c1": c1in})):
  +            r_ = dict(rec_, state_in=sin, inputs_in=iin, c1_in=c1in)
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(lay.gate("c3b", r_["gate"])), exist_ok=True)
  +            json.dump(r_, open(lay.gate("c3b", r_["gate"]), "w"))
  +        buf = io.StringIO()
  +        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf):

  +    def t_c3b_cli():
  +        """Review finding 3: the three c3b gates to success through the `run` CLI on a temporary node root with the
  +        scripts/46 Layout (the only path the node uses), with the inputs that scripts/50 gate_inputs writes. Only the
  +        status parser of scripts/47 is replaced (it has its own case, 'real parser'); the records are read by the
  +        scripts/46 loader. Also: the site table refused at the first gate (finding 2), the last point evaluated again
  +        (finding 6), the labels derived when the runner gives none, and verify on the root without its c1 tree."""
  +        import contextlib, io
  +        if mocked:
  +            return f"skipped ({', '.join(mocked)} absent)"
  +        c46, q47 = dep("c46"), dep("q47")
  +        root = os.path.join(work, "c3b_cli")
  +        lay = c46.Layout(kind="node", root=root)
  +        hosts = ["m06", "m02", "m16", "m12", "m32", "m26", "m22", "m23"]
  +        row = {"B": {"first": [124, 136], "far": [76, 172]}, "A": {"first": [28, 92, 128], "far": [188, 164]},
  +               "X": {"first": [3, 49, 53, 90, 102, 125, 126, 138], "far": [175, 186]}}
  +        sites = {h: copy.deepcopy(row) for h in hosts}
  +        state = {"n": 8, "G1": {"Cl": True, "Ca": False}, "pose": {"Cs": "default", "Cl": "default"},
  +                 "stage0": {"production_n8": hosts},
  +                 "campaigns": {"c1": "done", "c2": "done", "c3": "not_run", "fr": "done"}}
  +
  +        def save_state():
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(lay.state()), exist_ok=True)
  +            json.dump(state, open(lay.state(), "w"))
  +
  +        def touch_sp(c, lab):
  +            p = lay.sp_out(c, lab)
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
  +            open(p, "w").write("synthetic single-point output (status parser replaced)\n")
  +
  +        def put_record(c, wid, kind):
  +            v = {"valid": True, "failed": []}
  +            if kind != "host":
  +                v.update({"check5_reading": "invariant", "valid_as_written": True, "failed_as_written": [],
  +                          "invariant_only": False})
  +            p = lay.record(c, wid)
  +            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
  +            json.dump({"schema": "s2-cell-1", "work_id": wid, "cell_kind": kind, "validity": v,
  +                       "relax": {"wall_s": 7200.0 if kind == "host" else 600.0}}, open(p, "w"))
  +
  +        def fake_status(job_dir, label):
  +            if glob.glob(os.path.join(glob.escape(job_dir), f"{label}.out*")):
  +                return {"status": "complete", "wall_s": 1800.0}
  +            return {"status": "pending"}
  +
  +        def run(gate, inp, expect=0, dry=False):
  +            ip = os.path.join(root, "inputs.json")
  +            json.dump(inp, open(ip, "w"))
  +            buf = io.StringIO()
  +            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf), contextlib.redirect_stderr(buf):
  +                rc = main(["run", "--gate", gate, "--root", root, "--campaign", "c3b", "--inputs", ip] +
  +                          (["--dry-run"] if dry else []))
  +            assert rc == expect, (gate, rc, buf.getvalue()[-400:])
  +            if rc != 0:
  +                return buf.getvalue()
  +            if dry:
  +                return json.loads([x for x in buf.getvalue().splitlines() if x.startswith("DRY ")][-1][4:])
  +            return json.load(open(lay.gate("c3b", gate)))
  +        save_state()
  +        qs = c3b_quartets(sites)
  +        labels = c3b_table_labels(sites)
  +        wids = {q["wids"][r]: r for q in qs for r in ("host", "V", "D", "DV")}
  +        assert len(qs) == 8 * 24 and len(labels) == 544 and len(wids) == 544, (len(qs), len(labels), len(wids))
  +        for lab in labels:
  +            touch_sp("c3b", lab)
  +        for w, r in wids.items():
  +            put_record("c3b", w, r)
  +        for lab, w, k in (("s2_m30_host", "s2_m30_host", "host"), ("s2_m30_VB136", "s2_m30_VB136", "V")):
  +            touch_sp("c1", lab)                                      # the C1 timings of the projection (I-C7b)
  +            put_record("c1", w, k)
  +        proj = lambda pts: {"s2b_projection": {"points": pts, "cuts": [], "c4b_run": False,       # noqa: E731
  +                                               "c4b_origin": C4B_ORIGIN}}
  +        tab = {"c1": {"c3b_sites": sites}}
  +        orig = q47.sp_status
  +        q47.sp_status = fake_status
  +        try:
  +            # --dry-run (the pre-route check of scripts/50): the same decision, over_cap, and no gate file
  +            gdir = os.path.dirname(lay.gate("c3b", "s2b_projection"))

  +            assert d0 == {"gate": "s2b_projection", "campaign": "c3b", "decision": proj(["fr"]), "over_cap": False}, d0
  +            assert "site table" in run("s2b_projection", {"point": "fr", "H_spent_h": 150.0, "c1": {
  +                "c3b_sites": {h: sites[h] for h in hosts[:-1]}}}, 3, dry=True)
  +            assert not os.path.isdir(gdir), "a dry run wrote a gate file"
  +            g1 = run("s2b_projection", {"point": "fr", "H_spent_h": 150.0, **tab})
  +            assert g1["decision"] == proj(["fr"]) and g1["result"]["over_cap"] is False, g1["decision"]
  +            assert g1["c1_in"] == {"c3b_sites": sites} and "site_table" not in g1["result"]
  +            assert g1["inputs_in"]["tau_SP_h"] == 0.5 and g1["inputs_in"]["host_wave_h"] == 2.0, g1["inputs_in"]
  +            assert g1["result"]["work"] == {"N_relax": 0, "N_SP": 544, "N_MACE": 536, "host_waves": 1}
  +            state.update(g1["decision"]); save_state()
  +            # finding 6: the point fr again (its marker lost): refused without the flag, the same decision with it
  +            assert "points recorded so far" in run("s2b_projection", {"point": "fr", "H_spent_h": 150.0, **tab}, 3)
  +            g1b = run("s2b_projection", {"point": "fr", "H_spent_h": 150.0, "reevaluate": True, **tab})
  +            assert g1b["decision"] == g1["decision"] and g1b["result"]["PU_h"] == g1["result"]["PU_h"]
  +            # the flag admits only the last recorded point: the point c3b is not yet recorded

  +                                                                      "reevaluate": True, **tab}, 3)
  +            # finding 2: a table without one host, or with a short first shell, is refused at this first gate
  +            short = copy.deepcopy(sites); short["m06"]["X"]["first"] = short["m06"]["X"]["first"][:-1]
  +            n_tab = 0
  +            for bad in ({h: sites[h] for h in hosts[:-1]}, short):

  +                n_tab += 1
  +            g3 = run("c3b_complete", {"c1": {"c3b_labels": labels}})
  +            assert g3["decision"] == {"c3b_complete": True}, g3["decision"]
  +            g3b = run("c3b_complete", {})                           # no labels given: derived from the node tree
  +            assert g3b["decision"] == g3["decision"] and g3b["inputs_in"]["c1"]["c3b_labels"] == labels
  +            os.remove(lay.sp_out("c3b", labels[0]))
  +            g3c = run("c3b_complete", {"c1": {"c3b_labels": labels}})

  +def c3b_block4_reason(text):
  +    """N-C3B3 (review finding 8): None iff the text of E's block 4 assigns BLOCK4_IMPLEMENTED exactly once and that

  +    def _git(self, args, what):
  +        try:
  +            return subprocess.run(["git", "-C", self.node.checkout] + list(args), stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
  +                                  stderr=subprocess.PIPE, universal_newlines=True, timeout=600)
  +        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
  +            raise Stop(f"git {what} failed in the checkout ({type(e).__name__}): nothing started (N-FR2)")
  +
  +    def git_is_ancestor(self, a, b):
  +        """N-FR2: True iff commit a is an ancestor of b (git merge-base --is-ancestor); any other exit is a STOP."""
  +        p = self._git(["merge-base", "--is-ancestor", str(a), str(b)], "merge-base")
  +        if p.returncode not in (0, 1):
  +            raise Stop(f"git merge-base --is-ancestor exited {p.returncode}: nothing started (N-FR2)")
  +        return p.returncode == 0
  +
  +    def git_tracked(self, path):
  +        """N-FR2: True iff path is a tracked file of the checkout (git ls-files --error-unmatch)."""
  +        return self._git(["ls-files", "--error-unmatch", "--", path], "ls-files").returncode == 0
  +
  +    def git_unchanged(self, a, b, paths):
  +        """N-FR2: True iff the paths are equal at commits a and b (git diff --quiet); any other exit is a STOP."""
  +        p = self._git(["diff", "--quiet", str(a), str(b), "--"] + list(paths), "diff")
  +        if p.returncode not in (0, 1):
  +            raise Stop(f"git diff --quiet exited {p.returncode}: nothing started (N-FR2)")
  +        return p.returncode == 0
  +
  +    def git_changed_paths(self, a, b):
  +        """N-FR2: the paths that differ between commits a and b (git diff --name-only); a failure is a STOP."""
  +        p = self._git(["diff", "--name-only", str(a), str(b)], "diff")
  +        if p.returncode != 0:
  +            raise Stop(f"git diff --name-only exited {p.returncode}: nothing started (N-FR2)")
  +        return [x for x in p.stdout.splitlines() if x]
  +

  +        return (f"{self.c}: the protected state key(s) {', '.join(self.protected_changes(s)) or '(not identified)'} "
  +                f"changed; an S2b runner writes only {', '.join(S2B_WRITES[self.c])}, the {self.c} entry of campaigns and "
  +                f"new commit_history entries: state.json is not written (N-FR4)")
  +
  +    @staticmethod
  +    def protected_parts(s, n, c="fr"):
  +        """N-FR4: {name: canonical JSON} of every protected part of s, for naming what changed. c: the S2b campaign
  +        whose runner asks (N-C3B2); for c3b the campaign_commit entries of the other campaigns are protected too."""
  +        keep = {k: v for k, v in s.items() if k not in S2B_WRITES[c] + ("campaigns", "commit_history")}
  +        out = {k: json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True, default=str) for k, v in keep.items()}
  +        out.update({f"campaigns.{k}": json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True, default=str)
  +                    for k, v in (s.get("campaigns") or {}).items() if k != c})
  +        if c not in FR_ONLY:
  +            out.update({f"campaign_commit.{k}": json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True, default=str)
  +                        for k, v in (s.get("campaign_commit") or {}).items() if k != c})
  +        hist = list(s.get("commit_history") or [])
  +        out.update({f"commit_history[{i}]": json.dumps(x, sort_keys=True, default=str) for i, x in enumerate(hist[:n])})
  +        if len(hist) < n:
  +            out["commit_history (entries removed)"] = str(len(hist))
  +        return out
  +
  +    def protected_changes(self, s):
  +        """N-FR4: the names of the protected parts of s that differ from the state read at load_state."""
  +        if self.protect is None or self.protect_parts is None:
  +            return []
  +        now = self.protected_parts(s, self.protect[0], self.c)
  +        return sorted(k for k in set(now) | set(self.protect_parts) if now.get(k) != self.protect_parts.get(k))
  +
  +    @staticmethod
  +    def protected(s, n=None, c="fr"):
  +        """N-FR4: (number of commit_history entries at load, digest of every key outside the keys campaign c writes
  +        (S2B_WRITES), campaigns and commit_history, of campaigns without the entry of c and of those commit_history
  +        entries). For fr this is the digest of 0a3e8f0; for c3b it also covers the campaign_commit entries of the
  +        other campaigns (N-C3B2)."""
  +        hist = list(s.get("commit_history") or [])
  +        n = len(hist) if n is None else n
  +        keep = {k: v for k, v in s.items() if k not in S2B_WRITES[c] + ("campaigns", "commit_history")}
  +        camp = {k: v for k, v in (s.get("campaigns") or {}).items() if k != c}
  +        parts = [keep, camp, hist[:n], len(hist) >= n]
  +        if c not in FR_ONLY:
  +            parts.append({k: v for k, v in (s.get("campaign_commit") or {}).items() if k != c})
  +        blob = json.dumps(parts, sort_keys=True, default=str)
  +        return n, hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()
  +
